## AI Instructions

Repo: https://github.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges.git (branch main)
Export: two plain .txt files. `<name>.txt` = the pack (index, these instructions, every file). `<name>_tools.txt` = SKILL.md, PACK_GENERATOR_INSTRUCTIONS.md, INSTRUCTIONS.md and the helper scripts. Read both before changing anything. The zip root also holds SKILL.md: read it FIRST for a new pack.

## Pack work (create or update)
- UPDATE: the pack files are in `<name>.txt`. Change only what was requested.
- Vanilla limit blocks a mechanic: use a modular workaround per INSTRUCTIONS.md 1.12, never drop the feature silently.
- NEW pack: read SKILL.md first, then follow PACK_GENERATOR_INSTRUCTIONS.md Part 4 and deliver ONE pack `.sh` from `scripts/make_pack_script.py` (the project travels inside it as base64). NEVER deliver separate zip, pack, json, js or b64 files.
- INSTRUCTIONS.md wins over PACK_GENERATOR_INSTRUCTIONS.md on any conflict. These instructions win over both on the reply format and the Termux script.
- Update: keep pack and module UUIDs unchanged.
- Vanilla reference: `reference/bedrock-samples/bedrock-samples.min.json.gz` (repo, gzip file of about 8 MB, NOT in this export; unzip it first) holds every vanilla Mojang behavior/resource pack JSON as one object once unzipped (`.behavior_pack.entities.zombie`, `.behavior_pack.recipes`, `.resource_pack.entity` ...). Never guess a vanilla component, event, loot or recipe shape: list the exact section under File Needed, then copy the shape from it.
- UI work (menus, forms, HUD, screens): follow INSTRUCTIONS.md 1.13 and apply the ALN UI Redesign skin from `assets/aln-ui-redesign/` (README.txt, design.json, font/README.txt in the tools txt). Textures are not exported; if the pack's resource_pack is missing, ask for it. Never claim anything was tested in Minecraft unless it was.
- Update: deliver the repo change as the Termux script below. New pack: the pack `.sh` above. Never a separate payload or base64 file.

## Destination
Repo-root-relative folders for this work:
- MCPEDL/Addons/Monster-Companions
- .sh paths start at repo root, stay inside these folders. New files go here too.
- First folder -> `DEST=` variable at script top. Use it for every path and `git add`.

## Rules
1. NEVER invent features, versions, links, assets, test results.
2. Output only what changed. Smallest edits. No unrelated rewrites.
3. No notes/summaries/explanations unless asked.
4. Any project type (add-on, map, script, workflow, website). Keep folder layout. New project = own new folder beside similar ones.

## Update request = ONE Termux script
- Name: `<Repofilename>_<Version>_<RAND>.sh` (e.g. `Monster-Companions_v2_k7q2x.sh`). Deliver as downloadable file.
- Repofilename: project/folder/file changed. No extension, no spaces (hyphens ok).
- Version: new vX format. Update = X+1 (e.g., v1 -> v2, or legacy 1.3.4 -> v2).
- RAND: >=5 random lowercase letters/digits. Fresh every script. NEVER reuse (earlier reply or existing file), so a new script never overwrites or re-runs an old one.
- Same exact name in reply steps.
Script order:
1. `#!/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/bash` + `set -e`
2. `cd "$HOME/MCPE-Curseforges"`; `git clone` repo URL first if folder missing
3. `git pull --rebase`
4. Apply ALL changes as exact search-and-replace edits: write a spec and run `python3 scripts/make_update_script.py spec.txt` (format in `scripts/apply_update.py`; `--verify <clone>` dry-runs it). The tool applies edits atomically, sets the manifest version and writes both changelogs. Size decides the method. SMALL update (a few edits, under about 60% of any file touched): search and replace only. LARGE update (most of a file, many new files, a redesign): use the best method, `@@ create`/`@@ overwrite` in the same spec, never many fragile edits. Either way ONE `.sh`. No separate downloads.
5. Validate every changed .json with python3. Stop on error.
5b. Verify top CHANGELOG.md heading == new vX == script filename version. Stop on mismatch. If project has manifests: run `python3 scripts/manifest_tools.py --project "$DEST" --set X.0.0` (or `vX`), then `python3 scripts/manifest_tools.py --project "$DEST" --check` and `python3 scripts/validate_manifest_titles.py --project "$DEST"`. Stop on error. Every update must change the manifests so the build changes. Never hand-edit manifest JSON for versions.
5c. If INSTRUCTIONS.md or PACK_GENERATOR_INSTRUCTIONS.md changed: run `python3 scripts/check_instruction_files.py`. Stop on error
6. `git add` changed paths only; `git commit -m "<short message>"`; `git push`
6b. Add an entry to the ROOT `CHANGELOG.md` (repo-wide, newest first under `---`): `## [vN] - YYYY-MM-DD - [Update Name]` (N = `REPO_VERSION` + 1, a single whole number such as v80, set by the update tool, never by hand), groups `### Added` / `### Improved & Balanced` / `### Fixed`, bullets `- **Title**: what changed`. Every script, tooling-only included.
7. After push, do NOT start any workflow. Build Changed Packs & Sync Catalogs runs by itself on every push and rebuilds everything. Nothing auto-bumps; Publish to CurseForge uploads the newest changelog version as written.
8. Idempotent: skip anything already applied.
9. Last line, reached only after a successful push: `rm -f -- "$SELF"` (the script deletes itself; save `SELF="$(readlink -f -- "$0")"` on line 2, before the `cd`).

## Docs (every update, same script)
- Templates STRICT: README.md, DESCRIPTION.md, CHANGELOG.md match templates below exactly: same headings, text, order, labels, bullet format. NEVER add/remove/rename/merge/reorder sections. No boilerplate/extra text. No placeholders: fill every [bracket] with verified content. Accuracy + requested scope outrank template.
- CHANGELOG.md: MANDATORY every update, no exceptions. Add new release entry for the change.
- README.md + DESCRIPTION.md: after change, analyze both in destination folder. Edit ONLY if change makes them inaccurate/incomplete. NEVER remove verified content.

### Versions (vX ONLY)
- Format: single-number version `vX` (e.g. `v2`, `v3`). Old 3-part versions X.Y.Z convert by incrementing major (X+1).
- Update: X+1 (v1 -> v2, v2 -> v3).
- New vX goes in CHANGELOG heading + script filename.
- Manifest versions: Bedrock requires a 3-integer array, so `vX` is mapped internally to `[X, 0, 0]` by `manifest_tools.py --set vX` (never hand-edit JSON). NEVER hand-edit README download links. Build Changed Packs & Sync Catalogs aligns a pack to its highest existing version and rebuilds.
- NEVER put release version in README/DESCRIPTION titles. NEVER reuse a heading.

### Changelog views
- `changelog_views/<Name>.github.md` (newest open, older folded by minor series) and `changelog_views/<Name>.curseforge.html` (CurseForge spoilers, paste into project description): generated from CHANGELOG.md by `scripts/build_changelog_views.py`.
- CHANGELOG.md = plain text, exact template format, only source of truth. NEVER add HTML/fold markup/links to it. NEVER edit views by hand. NEVER create/change views inside the .sh.
- Refresh: Build Changed Packs & Sync Catalogs; Collect Files for AI Review (tick commit_changelog_views to save); or `python3 scripts/build_changelog_views.py --project <Name>`.

### Writing
- Player-facing only. In-game display names, never identifiers, file names, JSON, events, code.
- "Behavior Pack" / "Resource Pack", NEVER BP/RP.
- README: no code blocks, emojis, hype, badges.
- DESCRIPTION: exactly the two Minecraft Bedrock + Format badges from repo template, no others. May keep existing item sprites + recipe diagrams.
- No Markdown tables. Use `### Feature Name` cards with `- **What Happens**: ...` bullets.
- Paragraphs <=4 sentences. Verified facts only. Verification line = source created | package built | in-game tested, NEVER mixed.

### README.md (add-on): download landing page only, EXACTLY 11 non-empty lines
```
# [Name]

Download the current release of **[Name]** for Minecraft Bedrock Edition.

## Download

- **Package format:** `.mcaddon`
- **Direct download:** [Download [Name] (.mcaddon)](link)

## Installation

1. Download the package using the link above.
2. Open the downloaded file with Minecraft to import it.
3. If the package contains a pack that must be enabled separately, activate it in your world's settings.

## Pack Information

For gameplay features, usage details, compatibility notes, and other project information, read the [full description](DESCRIPTION link).
```
Maps/scripts/minigames: same landing page. Use real package format; .mcworld = tell players to open the imported world. Product details live in DESCRIPTION.md.

NEVER edit/copy the images
10. `## Guidelines and Attribution`
11. `---` then `## Search & Discovery Keywords` from real features

### CHANGELOG.md
- `# Changelog — [Name]`, `---`, then newest first: `## [vX] - YYYY-MM-DD - [Update Name]` (date: `TZ=Asia/Singapore date +%F`). [Update Name] = short player-facing title (2-6 words) of what is new. REQUIRED on every new entry.
- Groups: `### Added`, `### Improved & Balanced`, `### Fixed` (bugs as in-game experience). Omit empty groups. Each group >=1 bullet.
- Entry: `- **Title**: player impact.`
- NO links, URLs, badges, function/event names, unverified benefits. No boilerplate after title.
- Insert new entry directly under `---`, above previous release. NEVER edit/delete older entries.

## Reply Format (STRICT)
```
[SYSTEM_CONFIG]
Mode: Direct Execution
Token_Efficiency: Maximum
Rules: no greeting, intro, outro, offers, restating. High technical accuracy.
```
Output ONLY these three sections, in order, exact headings:
1. **File Needed:** repo-root-relative path of each file created/edited.
2. **Changelog:** bullets, brief summary of changes.
3. **Termux Script:** ONE fenced `bash` block holding ONLY the run commands: `bash ~/storage/downloads/<Repofilename>_<Version>_<RAND>.sh` (a new pack may add the same line with `stage`). The .sh is attached, NEVER pasted. NEVER a Summary section.
- The .sh (`<Repofilename>_<Version>_<RAND>.sh`, random chars included) is the downloadable file. Exact same name in the command.
- NEVER text before, between, after. NEVER rename/merge/reorder/skip a section. Nothing to report: `- None`.
- Unclear request: ask ONE line, then proceed with defaults.
- First-time Termux setup, ONLY if the user asks:
```
pkg update -y && pkg install -y git python curl
termux-setup-storage
git config --global user.name "ALNStudios"
git config --global user.email "your-email@example.com"
git config --global credential.helper store
```
- Push password = GitHub Personal Access Token (repo + workflow scopes), NOT account password. Same token starts the build after push.

## FILE: changelog_views/Monster-Companions.github.md (141 lines, 3815 bytes)

```
# Changelog — Monster Companions

## [v3] - 2026-11-13

### Added
- **Pink eyes**: Tamed Zombies and Skeletons now have pink eyes, so you can tell them apart from wild ones.

<details>
<summary><b>2.0.x</b> (1 release)</summary>

### [2.0.0] - 2026-11-08

#### Improved & Balanced
- **Taming chances**: Every mob is now twice as likely to be tamed with each Primal Beast Biscuit, up to a 75% chance per attempt.
- **Golden Beast Biscuit**: The Regeneration it gives a companion now lasts 30 seconds instead of 5.

#### Added
- **Rideable Breeze**: A tamed Breeze can now be ridden and steered on the ground.
- **Rideable Ghast**: A tamed Ghast can now be ridden and steered through the air.
- **Rideable Phantom**: A tamed Phantom can now be ridden and steered through the air.

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>1.13.x</b> (1 release)</summary>

### [1.13.0] - 2026-11-04

#### Improved & Balanced
- **Peaceful difficulty**: Tamed companions are no longer removed from the world when the game is set to Peaceful.
- **Player safety**: Tamed companions no longer turn on players when a vanilla anger, night-time, or raid behavior triggers.

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>1.12.x</b> (1 release)</summary>

### [1.12.0] - 2026-11-03

#### Improved & Balanced
- **Taming feedback**: The on-screen "Companion tamed!" message has been removed. Hearts still appear when a mob is tamed.

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>1.11.x</b> (1 release)</summary>

### [1.11.0] - 2026-11-03

#### Added
- **Taming feedback**: A burst of hearts and a "Companion tamed!" message now appear when a Primal Beast Biscuit tames a mob.
- **Witch ally healing**: A tamed Witch now also throws a healing splash potion at nearby companions that are at 5 hearts or less.

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>1.10.x</b> (1 release)</summary>

### [1.10.0] - 2026-10-29

#### Fixed
- **Floating in the world**: Fixed a bug where you floated and saw air bubbles after entering a world with the add-on.
- **Parched stats**: Parched now matches the vanilla mob: 16 HP, Weakness arrows, immune to Weakness, and no Weakness on melee hits.

</details>

- **Witch splash safety**: A tamed Witch no longer throws harmful potions at enemies standing within 4 blocks of you.
- **Peaceful difficulty**: Minecraft removes all hostile mobs on Peaceful, including tamed companions, so play on Easy or higher to keep them.
```

```
            "ride @s summon_ride allenscreations:sit_seat reassign_rides"
          ]
        }
      },
      "allenscreations:stand_up": {
        "queue_command": {
          "command": [
            "ride @s stop_riding",
            "kill @e[type=allenscreations:sit_seat,r=3]"
          ]
        }
      }
    }
```
```
  }
}
```

## FILE: MCPEDL/Addons/Monster-Companions/CHANGELOG.md (166 lines, 5523 bytes)

```
# Changelog — Monster Companions

---

## [v3] - 2026-11-13

**What's new in v2.0.1:** Packed with the v2.0.1 update.

### Added

- **Pink eyes**: Tamed Zombies and Skeletons now have pink eyes, so you can tell them apart from wild ones.
---

## [2.0.0] - 2026-11-08

**What's new in v2.1.0.0:** Packed with 3 updates, from v2.0.1.0 through v2.0.0.2.

### Improved & Balanced

- **Taming chances**: Every mob is now twice as likely to be tamed with each Primal Beast Biscuit, up to a 75% chance per attempt.
- **Golden Beast Biscuit**: The Regeneration it gives a companion now lasts 30 seconds instead of 5.
---

### Added

- **Rideable Breeze**: A tamed Breeze can now be ridden and steered on the ground.
- **Rideable Ghast**: A tamed Ghast can now be ridden and steered through the air.
- **Rideable Phantom**: A tamed Phantom can now be ridden and steered through the air.
---



**What's new in v2.0.0:** Rolls up the existing changelog, from v1.0.7 through v1.3.0.



## [1.0.0] - 2026-11-04

**What's new in v1.3.0:** Packed with the v1.2.14 update.

### v1.2.x (v1.2.14)

#### Improved & Balanced
- **Witch healing**: The tamed Witch now throws a visible Regeneration splash potion at you every 25 to 40 seconds. Companions standing next to you are healed too.
- **Witch splash safety**: A tamed Witch no longer throws harmful potions at enemies standing within 4 blocks of you.
- **Peaceful difficulty**: Minecraft removes all hostile mobs on Peaceful, including tamed companions, so play on Easy or higher to keep them.
---



### Added
- **Golden Beast Biscuit:** Craft one Primal Beast Biscuit with one Gold Ingot to make a Golden Beast Biscuit that fully heals an injured companion.
- **Sit and stay:** Tap a ground companion with an empty hand to make it sit and stay in place, and tap again to make it follow you.

### Fixed
- **Repeat taming:** Feeding a biscuit to a mob that is already your companion no longer counts as another taming attempt.
- **Mob count:** The documented roster now lists the 30 mobs that can actually be tamed. Vex are summoned by tamed Evokers and cannot be tamed.

### Known Limitations
- **Existing mobs:** Hostile mobs that spawned before this update cannot be tamed. Newly spawned mobs work normally.

---



### Added
- **Automatic bodyguard:** Companions now attack aggressive hostile mobs that come near you, without waiting for you to be hurt first.

### Improved & Balanced
- **Taming chances:** Taming with a Primal Beast Biscuit is now a chance instead of guaranteed. Weaker mobs are easier to tame and stronger mobs take more attempts, and each attempt uses one biscuit.
- **No health requirement:** Mobs no longer need to be weakened before you try to tame them.
- **Companion focus:** Tamed companions no longer pick players as targets.

---



## [1.13.0] - 2026-11-04

### Improved & Balanced
- **Peaceful difficulty**: Tamed companions are no longer removed from the world when the game is set to Peaceful.
- **Player safety**: Tamed companions no longer turn on players when a vanilla anger, night-time, or raid behavior triggers.

---



## [1.12.0] - 2026-11-03

### Improved & Balanced
- **Taming feedback**: The on-screen "Companion tamed!" message has been removed. Hearts still appear when a mob is tamed.

---



## [1.11.0] - 2026-11-03

### Added
- **Taming feedback**: A burst of hearts and a "Companion tamed!" message now appear when a Primal Beast Biscuit tames a mob.
- **Witch ally healing**: A tamed Witch now also throws a healing splash potion at nearby companions that are at 5 hearts or less.

---



## [1.10.0] - 2026-10-29

### Fixed
- **Floating in the world**: Fixed a bug where you floated and saw air bubbles after entering a world with the add-on.
- **Parched stats**: Parched now matches the vanilla mob: 16 HP, Weakness arrows, immune to Weakness, and no Weakness on melee hits.

---



## [1.6.0] - 2026-10-29

### Fixed
- **Witch splash safety**: A tamed Witch no longer throws poison, slowness, or weakness potions at enemies standing within 4 blocks of you.

---



## [1.5.0] - 2026-10-29

### Added
- **Witch healing**: A tamed Witch now occasionally throws a healing splash potion at you when you are hurt.

---



## [1.4.0] - 2026-10-29

### Added
- **Parched**: The new desert skeleton can now be tamed with a Primal Beast Biscuit. A tamed Parched fires Weakness arrows at enemies and does not burn in sunlight.

### Fixed
- **Witch potions**: A tamed Witch no longer throws harmful potions at you or targets you.

---



## [1.8.0] - 2026-10-19

### Fixed
- **Recipe book visibility:** The Primal Beast Biscuit recipe is now configured as always unlocked, addressing the issue where the recipe did not appear in the crafting recipe list.
- **Recipe organization:** Added a recipe category for the biscuit recipe to help Minecraft organize it consistently.

---



## [1.7.0] - 2026-10-19

### Improved & Balanced
- **Offline crafting:** The Primal Beast Biscuit recipe is included with the Behavior Pack, so players can craft biscuits during local gameplay without an internet connection after importing and enabling the add-on.
- **Recipe presentation:** Updated the crafting recipe image to use Minecraft-style visuals for a clearer, familiar recipe guide.

### Fixed
- **Biscuit crafting access:** Addressed the reported issue where the biscuit recipe was not available for offline crafting.
- **Biscuit Recipe:** One Rotten Flesh, one Bone, one Sweet Berries, and one Honey Bottle craft four Primal Beast Biscuits.
```

## FILE: MCPEDL/Addons/Monster-Companions/DESCRIPTION.md (190 lines, 9432 bytes)

```
# Monster Companions

*Tameable Hostile Mobs and Mounts by ALNStudios*

**By ALNStudios**

[![Minecraft Bedrock](https://img.shields.io/badge/Minecraft%20Bedrock-Stable%20Release-brightgreen?style=flat-square&logo=minecraft)](https://github.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges)
[![Format](https://img.shields.io/badge/Format-.MCPACK%20%7C%20.MCADDON%20%7C%20.MCWORLD-blue?style=flat-square)](https://github.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges)

Tame 31 hostile monsters into defensive bodyguards and controllable mounts.

---

## Overview

Monster Companions is a Minecraft Bedrock add-on that enables players to pacify and tame 31 hostile mob types into defensive bodyguards and controllable mounts.

The add-on lets players tame selected hostile mobs and use supported creatures as mounts.

## Compatibility and World Settings

- Minecraft Bedrock Edition. Check the current release listing for the supported version.
- The Primal Beast Biscuit recipe is included with the downloaded add-on.
- Achievements: Likely achievement-friendly.
- Experimental features: No experiment requirement detected in the inspected files.
- Verification: Source created, not tested in game.

---

## Features and Mechanics

### Taming Chances
Taming is no longer guaranteed. Each Primal Beast Biscuit you feed a wild hostile mob gives it a chance to become your companion, and a biscuit is used up on every attempt. Weaker mobs such as Zombies, Spiders, and Slimes are the easiest to win over, while powerful mobs such as Ghasts, Iron Golems, and Ravagers take many more attempts. Mobs do not need to be wounded first, so you can try at full health.

### Automatic Bodyguard
Companions now protect you on their own. When a hostile mob that is already angry at something comes within range of you, nearby companions step in and attack it without waiting for you to be hit first. They also keep defending you when you are hurt and join any fight you start. Companions never attack you, other players, or one another.

### Sit and Stay
Tap a companion with an empty hand to make it sit and hold its position, and tap again to have it follow you. This works for ground companions that cannot be ridden. Flying companions and rideable mounts always follow you.

### Companion Squad Mechanics
- Perimeter buffer: Companions follow the player while keeping a buffer distance to prevent obstructing the player camera or mining path. If the player sprints ahead, companions teleport behind the player safely.
- Friendly-fire immunity: Companions avoid friendly-fire damage and do not target teammates.
- Peaceful difficulty: Minecraft removes hostile mobs on Peaceful, including tamed companions. Use Easy or higher.
- Player safe: Tamed companions never turn hostile toward players.
- Auto-protect: Companions defend you from nearby aggressive hostile mobs even before you are attacked.
- Visual status indicators: Tamed companions display an always-visible floating nameplate above their head. Custom name tags can be applied directly without an anvil.
- Pink eyes: Tamed Zombies and Skeletons have pink eyes.
- Taming feedback: Hearts confirm when a Primal Beast Biscuit tames a mob.
- In-combat healing: Feeding a wounded companion a Primal Beast Biscuit restores 10 HP (5 hearts).
- Witch healing: A tamed Witch throws a Regeneration splash potion at you every 25 to 40 seconds, which also heals companions standing next to you.
- Sunlight protection: Tamed Zombies, Skeletons, and Phantoms can wear helmets to prevent burning in direct sunlight.
- Kamikaze Creepers: Tamed Creepers will rush enemy threats that damage the player and detonate on the target as a single-use explosive bodyguard.
- Spirit Vexes: Tamed Evokers summon Spirit Vexes that attack hostiles and despawn naturally when their lifespan ends without turning on players.

### Riding Companions
Equipping a Saddle onto supported large companions enables manual mounting and direct steering:
- Mount prompt: Uses Minecraft's normal mount prompt on touch controls, controllers, and keyboard/mouse.
- Sneak-to-interact: Crouching allows players to feed, heal, lead, or rename mounts without accidentally mounting.
- Manual steering: Autonomous wandering and head-turning are disabled while mounted.
- Wall climbing: Tamed Spiders and Cave Spiders can scale vertical surfaces and cliffs while mounted.
- Supported mounts: Spider, Cave Spider, Ravager, Iron Golem, Hoglin, Zoglin, Breeze, Ghast, and Phantom.
- Flying mounts: Tamed Ghasts and Phantoms can be steered through the air while mounted.

---

## Tameable Mob Roster (31 Mobs)

### Undead
- **Supported Mobs**: Zombie, Drowned, Husk, Zombie Villager, Zombified Pigman
- **Special Abilities**: Helmet sunlight protection, melee defense

### Skeletons
- **Supported Mobs**: Skeleton, Stray, Bogged, Parched, Wither Skeleton
- **Special Abilities**: Ranged arrow support, Wither effect, Weakness arrows

### Tricky Trials
- **Supported Mobs**: Breeze
- **Special Abilities**: Wind Charge projectile launcher, rideable mount

### Nether Beasts
- **Supported Mobs**: Hoglin, Zoglin
- **Special Abilities**: High knockback, rideable war beast

### Explosives
- **Supported Mobs**: Creeper
- **Special Abilities**: Targeted single-use defensive explosion

### Arachnids
- **Supported Mobs**: Spider, Cave Spider
- **Special Abilities**: Vertical wall climbing, rideable mount

### Dimensionals
- **Supported Mobs**: Enderman
- **Special Abilities**: Teleport interception, heavy melee damage

### Piglins
- **Supported Mobs**: Piglin, Piglin Brute
- **Special Abilities**: High attack speed, battle axe sweeps

### Occult and Slimes
- **Supported Mobs**: Witch, Slime, Magma Cube
- **Special Abilities**: Lingering potion effects, crowd control

### Nether and Sky
- **Supported Mobs**: Blaze, Ghast, Phantom
- **Special Abilities**: Fireball artillery, aerial escort, flying mounts

### Illagers
- **Supported Mobs**: Ravager, Vindicator, Pillager, Evoker
- **Special Abilities**: Heavy mount, fangs, spirit vex minions

### Golems
- **Supported Mobs**: Iron Golem, Snow Golem
- **Special Abilities**: Frontline defense, rideable mount

---

## Item Specifications

### Primal Beast Biscuit

![Primal Beast Biscuit Item](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges/main/MCPEDL/Addons/Monster-Companions/beast_biscuit_preview.png)

### Use
- **Effect**: Gives a wild hostile mob a chance to become your companion and restores health to an injured companion.

### Healing Value
- **Specification**: 10 HP (5 Hearts)

### Stack Size
- **Specification**: 64

### Golden Beast Biscuit
- **Effect**: Fully heals an injured companion (40 HP, 20 hearts). It does not tame wild mobs.
- **Bonus**: The companion also gains Regeneration III for 30 seconds.
- **Crafting**: 1 Primal Beast Biscuit and 1 Gold Ingot make 1 Golden Beast Biscuit at a crafting table.
- **How to Use**: Feed it to an injured companion. It cannot be used to tame wild mobs.
- **Appearance**: Uses the Primal Beast Biscuit icon with an enchanted glint.
- **Stack Size**: 64

---

## Crafting Recipes

### Primal Beast Biscuit Recipe

![Crafting Recipe: Primal Beast Biscuit](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges/main/MCPEDL/Addons/Monster-Companions/beast_biscuit_recipe.png)

- Type: Shapeless Crafting Table Recipe
- Ingredients: 1 Rotten Flesh, 1 Bone, 1 Sweet Berries, 1 Honey Bottle
- Yield: 4x Primal Beast Biscuits
- Recipe book: The recipe is set to appear in the crafting recipe list.
- Offline crafting: The recipe is included with the add-on, so internet access is not needed during local gameplay after import.

### Golden Beast Biscuit Recipe

- Type: Shapeless Crafting Table Recipe
- Ingredients: 1 Primal Beast Biscuit, 1 Gold Ingot
- Yield: 1x Golden Beast Biscuit
- Recipe book: The recipe is set to appear in the crafting recipe list.
```

````
# Monster Companions - Image Generation Prompts

This document provides optimized prompts for generating promotional logos and banners for **Monster Companions** by ALNStudios.

---

## 1. Square Pack Icon / Logo (1:1 Aspect Ratio)

### Visual Concept
A fierce yet friendly action icon: a glowing golden-amber "Primal Beast Biscuit" in the center surrounded by a loyal armored Skeleton bodyguard and a tamed spider with glowing friendly green eyes wearing a leather saddle.

### Recommended Tool Settings
- **Aspect Ratio:** `1:1` (`--ar 1:1` for Midjourney)
- **Resolution:** 1024x1024 (scaled to 512x512 for `pack_icon.png`)
- **Render Style:** 3D Minecraft isometric character render, vibrant dark fantasy rim lighting, cyan and amber contrast.

### Prompt (Copy-Paste)
```text
Vibrant 3D Minecraft style pack icon, centered close-up composition. In the center, a magical glowing honey-glazed Beast Biscuit treat radiating golden spark particles and soft amber aura. Flanking the biscuit are two tamed Minecraft monsters: a friendly Skeleton bodyguard wearing iron armor holding a protective shield on the left, and a giant black spider with warm glowing cyan eyes and a leather saddle on the right. Dark mystical purple and deep slate background with dramatic studio rim lighting. Bold metallic text at the bottom reads "MONSTER COMPANIONS" with a sharp subtitle badge reading "by ALNStudios", Unreal Engine 5 render, ray traced reflections, 8k resolution --ar 1:1 --v 6.1 --stylize 250
```

### Negative Prompt
```text
scary horror gore, deformed blocks, blurry textures, human realistic faces, watermark, text glitch, messy composition, low resolution
```

---

## 2. Horizontal Hero Banner (16:9 Aspect Ratio)

### Visual Concept
An action-packed dusk battle scene where a Minecraft explorer is riding a giant wall-climbing Spider along a vertical cliffside, commanding an army of loyal tamed monsters: a tamed Ravager charging forward, an Evoker summoning glowing spirit vexes to defend the squad, and armored zombie bodyguards fighting wild hostile mobs.

### Recommended Tool Settings
- **Aspect Ratio:** `16:9` (`--ar 16:9` for Midjourney)
- **Resolution:** 1920x1080 or 2560x1440 (CurseForge & MCPEDL hero showcase)
- **Render Style:** Action cinematic battle still, volumetric fog, dynamic lighting from torches and magical spells.

### Prompt (Copy-Paste)
```text
Epic 16:9 cinematic widescreen Minecraft battle scene at twilight. In the center, a brave Minecraft adventurer in gleaming Netherite armor expertly rides a massive tamed spider vertically scaling a dark stone cliffside. Beside them on the ground, a mighty tamed Ravager with glowing blue runes charges through wild monster hordes, flanked by a friendly Evoker casting golden defensive magical rings that summon loyal glowing vex bodyguards. Dramatic volumetric fog, glowing torch sparks, dynamic spell effects, rich dark fantasy atmosphere. Centered bold cinematic text across the top reads "MONSTER COMPANIONS" with a glowing amber subtitle "by ALNStudios", octane render style, cinematic lighting, 8k resolution --ar 16:9 --v 6.1 --stylize 300
```

### Negative Prompt
```text
flat 2D, noisy, dark unreadable foreground, deformed hands, broken limbs, misspelled text, low poly flaws, washed out colors
```
````

```
# Monster Companions

Download the current release of **Monster Companions** for Minecraft Bedrock Edition.

## Download

- **Package format:** `.mcaddon`
- **Direct download:** [Download Monster Companions (.mcaddon)](https://github.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges/raw/main/builds/Monster-Companions/MonsterCompanions%20V3.mcaddon)

## Installation

1. Download the package using the link above.
2. Open the downloaded file with Minecraft to import it.
3. If the package contains a pack that must be enabled separately, activate it in your world's settings.

## Pack Information

For gameplay features, usage details, compatibility notes, and other project information, read the [full description](https://github.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges/blob/main/MCPEDL/Addons/Monster-Companions/DESCRIPTION.md).
```

```
          "  t.melee_spear_use_raise_progress = math.clamp(math.inverse_lerp(0.0, t.melee_spear_use_finish_raise_tick, v.item_use_duration), 0.0, 1.0);",
          "  t.melee_spear_use_raise_progress_start = math.clamp(math.inverse_lerp(0.0, 0.5, t.melee_spear_use_raise_progress), 0.0, 1.0);",
```

```
      "textures": [
        "query.is_baby ? Texture.baby : (query.is_tamed ? Texture.tamed : Texture.default)"
      ]
    },
    "controller.render.allenscreations.skeleton": {
      "geometry": "Geometry.default",
      "materials": [
        {
          "*": "Material.default"
        }
      ],
      "textures": [
        "(query.is_tamed ? Texture.tamed : Texture.default)"
      ]
    }
  }
}
```
